			$ExceptionMessage = "$($Exception.Message) ($($ProcessName):$($ProcessId))"

			# in the case when 32-bit PowerShell tries to dump a 64-bit process.
			Remove-Item $ProcessDumpPath -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
